Unlike subjective accolades, this award is purely data-driven, honouring players who achieve their league or tournament’s highest average Sofascore Rating at the end of the season. Featuring a unique design inspired by the legendary Zico, Sofascore’s Player of the Season is the most transparent award in football.</p><h2>How winners are determined</h2><p>Sofascore crowns Player of the Season winners in over 150 global leagues and tournaments annually. Winners are determined by a sophisticated algorithm that processes thousands of data points from every match. Every action on the pitch—goals, assists, passes, interceptions, successful dribbles, to name just a few—contributes to a player’s average season rating. This objective approach ensures that the Player of the Season is a true reflection of sustained excellence, rewarding those who perform at the highest level week in, week out.</p><h2>Global recognition across major leagues</h2><p>Sofascore’s Player of the Season award is presented in over 150 professional football leagues worldwide. Physical trophies are sent to winners in the world's most elite major-league competitions, while in-app badges recognise winners across all participating leagues and tournaments.</p><p><strong>Key leagues and tournaments include:</strong></p><ul><li>Premier League (England)</li><li>La Liga (Spain)</li><li>Bundesliga (Germany)</li><li>UEFA Champions League</li><li>Serie A (Italy)</li><li>Ligue 1 (France)</li><li>Saudi Pro League (Saudi Arabia)</li><li>Brasileirão Betano (Brazil)</li><li>MLS (USA)</li><li>FIFA World Cup</li></ul><p><strong>Notable past Player of the Season winners:</strong></p><ul><li>Lionel Messi</li><li>Cristiano Ronaldo</li><li>Kylian Mbappé</li><li>Joshua Kimmich</li><li>Antoine Griezmann</li><li>Raphinha</li></ul>","
